833 entries, devp2p-peer-discovery to dew-fast. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **devp2p-peer-discovery**: Help users discover Ethereum peer addresses over devp2p when they don't have an enode:// URL yet — discv4 Kademlia UDP discovery, discv5 authenticated discovery, DNS ENR-tree bootstrapping (EIP-1459), or persisting a stable node identity/peer cache with Nethereum (.NET). Use this skill whenever the user mentions peer discovery, discv4, discv5, ENR, enode, finding Ethereum nodes, bootnodes, DNS discovery tree, or persisting a node key/peer list across restarts.
